Similarly, _Avatar_ views tribal people as an honest group, whereas a \"technologically advanced imperial culture is fundamentally evil\".[[178]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-179)[[179]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-180) The danger of nuclear war, as featured in _[The Terminator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\\_Terminator \"The Terminator\")_, _[Terminator 2: Judgement Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terminator\\_2:\\_Judgement\\_Day \"Terminator 2: Judgement Day\")_ and in his forthcoming _Last Train From Hiroshima_ film, has been one of Cameron's fears since he watched the [Cuban Missile Crisis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cuban_Missile_Crisis \"Cuban Missile Crisis\") unfold when he was eight years old.[[125]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-CameronsNextNonAvatarFilm!-126)\n\n### Method\n\n[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=James_Cameron&action=edit&section=12 \"Edit section: Method\")]\n\nCameron is regarded as an innovative filmmaker in the industry, with a [classical filmmaking](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classical_Hollywood_cinema \"Classical Hollywood cinema\") style, and a reputation of being not easy to work for.[[b]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-185)_[Radio Times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radio\\_Times \"Radio Times\")_ critic John Ferguson described Cameron as \"the king of hi-tech thrillers\".[[184]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-186) Dalin Rowell of _[/Film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki//Film \"/Film\")_ stated: \"Known for his larger-than-life creations and unique filmmaking style, director James Cameron is in a league all of his own. With his genre-spanning work, lofty ambitions, and unrestrained energy, Cameron has carved out a name for himself in Hollywood as an artist willing to do anything to see his vision come true.\"[[185]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-187) Rebecca Keegan, author of _The Futurist: The Life and Films of James Cameron_, describes Cameron as \"comically hands-on\", and as someone who would try to do every job on the set.[[181]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Keegan-2010-182) Andrew Gumbel of _[The Independent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\\_Independent \"The Independent\")_ says that Cameron \"is a nightmare to work with. Studios fear his habit of straying way over schedule and over budget. He is notorious on set for his uncompromising and dictatorial manner, as well as his flaming temper\".[[186]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-188) Author Alexandra Keller writes that Cameron is an egomaniac, obsessed with vision, but praises his \"technological ingenuity\" at creating a \"visceral viewing experience\".[[56]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Keller-2014-56)\n\nAccording to Ed Harris, who starred in Cameron's film _The Abyss_, Cameron behaved in an [autocratic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Autocracy \"Autocracy\") manner.[[34]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Harmetz-1989-34)[Orson Scott Card](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orson_Scott_Card \"Orson Scott Card\"), who novelized _The Abyss_, stated that Cameron \"made everyone around him miserable, and his unkindness did nothing to improve the film in any way. Nor did it motivate people to work faster or better\".[[187]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-189) Harris later said: \"I like Jim. He's an incredibly talented, intelligent guy\", adding that \"it was always good to see him\" in later years.[[188]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-190) Speaking of her experience on _Titanic_, Kate Winslet said that she admired Cameron, but \"there were times I was genuinely frightened of him\".[[189]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-191) Describing him as having \"a temper like you wouldn't believe\", she had said she would not work with him again unless it was \"for a lot of money\".[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-tim_walker-192) Despite this, Winslet and Cameron still looked for future projects and Winslet was eventually cast in _Avatar: The Way of Water_.[[191]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-193) Her co-star Leonardo DiCaprio told [_Esquire_](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Esquire_(magazine) \"Esquire (magazine)\"): \"When somebody felt a different way on the set, there was a confrontation. He lets you know exactly how he feels\", but complimented Cameron, \"he's of the lineage of [John Ford](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Ford \"John Ford\"). He knows what he wants his film to be.\"[[192]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-194) Sam Worthington, who starred in _Avatar_, said that if a mobile phone rang during filming, Cameron would \"nail it to the wall with a nail gun\".[[193]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-195) Composer James Horner was also not immune to Cameron's demands; he recalls having to write music in a short time frame for _Aliens._[[194]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-196) After the experience, Horner did not work with Cameron for a decade.[[195]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-197) In 1996, they reconciled their friendship and Horner produced the soundtracks for _Titanic_ and _Avatar_.[[196]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-198)\n\nDespite this reputation, Sigourney Weaver has praised Cameron's perfectionism and attention to detail, saying: \"He really does want us to risk our lives and limbs for the shot, but he doesn't mind risking his own\".[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-tim_walker-192) In 2015, Weaver and Jamie Lee Curtis both applauded Cameron in an interview. Curtis remarked: \"He can do every other job [than acting]. I'm talking about every single department, from art direction to props to wardrobe to cameras, he knows more than everyone doing the job\". Curtis also said Cameron \"loves actors\", while Weaver referred to Cameron as \"so generous to actors\" and a \"genius\".[[197]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-199) Michael Biehn, a frequent collaborator, also praised Cameron, saying that he \"is a really passionate person. He cares more about his movies than other directors care about their movies\", adding, \"I've never seen him yell at anybody\". Biehn acknowledged that Cameron is \"not real sensitive when it comes to actors and their trailers, and waiting for actors to come to the set\".[[198]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-200) Worthington commented: \"He demands excellence. If you don't give it to him, you're going to get chewed out. And that's a good thing\".[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-tim_walker-192) When asked in 2012 about his reputation, Cameron dryly responded: \"I don't have to shout any more, because the word is out there already\".[[199]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-201)\n\nIn 2021, while giving a [MasterClass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MasterClass \"MasterClass\") during a break from his work on the _Avatar_ sequels, Cameron acknowledged his past demanding behaviour, opining that if he could go back in time, he would improve the working relationship with his cast and crew members by being less autocratic, thinking of himself as a \"tinpot dictator\"; Cameron stated that when he visited one of [Ron Howard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ron_Howard \"Ron Howard\")'s sets, he was \"dumbfounded\" at how much time Howard took to compliment his crew, aspiring to become \"his inner Ron Howard\".[[200]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-202)\n\n### Influence\n\n[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=James_Cameron&action=edit&section=13 \"Edit section: Influence\")]\n\nCameron's work has had an impact in the Hollywood film industry. _[The Avengers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\\_Avengers\\_(2012\\_film) \"The Avengers (2012 film)\")_ (2012), directed by [Joss Whedon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joss_Whedon \"Joss Whedon\"), was inspired by Cameron's approach to action sequences.[[201]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Slash_Film-2012-203) Whedon also admires Cameron's ability for writing heroic female characters such as [Ellen Ripley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ellen_Ripley \"Ellen Ripley\") of _Aliens_,[[202]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-204) adding that he is \"the leader and the teacher and the [Yoda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoda \"Yoda\")\".[[201]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Slash_Film-2012-203) Director [Michael Bay](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Bay \"Michael Bay\") idolizes Cameron and was convinced by him to use 3D cameras for filming _[Transformers: Dark of the Moon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transformers:\\_Dark\\_of\\_the\\_Moon \"Transformers: Dark of the Moon\")_ (2011).[[203]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-205) Cameron's approach to 3D inspired [Baz Luhrmann](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baz_Luhrmann \"Baz Luhrmann\") during the production of _[The Great Gatsby](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\\_Great\\_Gatsby\\_(2013\\_film) \"The Great Gatsby (2013 film)\")_ (2013).[[204]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-206) Other directors that have been inspired by Cameron include [Peter Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Jackson \"Peter Jackson\"), [Neill Blomkamp](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neill_Blomkamp \"Neill Blomkamp\") and [Xavier Dolan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xavier_Dolan \"Xavier Dolan\").[[205]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-207)[[206]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-208)[[207]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-209)\n\nWhen asked how Cameron uses technology in the _Avatar_ films in a way that allow them to remain appealing to future generations without appearing dated, Cameron replied:[[177]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Collider12.5.25-178)\n\n> \"I think people use the term technology, but really, I think it's our desire to create a kind of dreamlike reality that seems very lucid and very real and doesn't look like other movies. So, it's really about creating that journey, and I think everything conspires within that. It's not just the technology we use to capture the actors, for example, or to do the CG finish on it. It's all those things, but it's also the performance, it's the design, it's the music, it's the 3D. It's all of those things working together, kind of symphonically, to create a unique experience that you, as an audience member, feel is worth turning off your streaming, leaving the house, going to a place where you don't have control, and just going on that ride and having it come at you.\"[[177]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Collider12.5.25-178)\n\nCameron advises modern filmmakers to not replace actors with [artificial intelligence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artificial_intelligence \"Artificial intelligence\"), reasoning that while actors may be expensive, they are not generic but idiosyncratic. Cameron instead prescribes the use of artificial intelligence to be limited to beginners' [previsualization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Previsualization \"Previsualization\"), and that filmmakers should spend at least a year studying acting, citing his experiences with Sigourney Weaver and [Zoe Saldaña](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zoe_Salda%C3%B1a \"Zoe Saldaña\") as the reasons why he trusts actors.[[177]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Collider12.5.25-178)\n\n## recognition\n\n[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=James_Cameron&action=edit&section=16 \"Edit section: Awards and recognition\")]\n\nMain article: [List of awards and nominations received by James Cameron](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_awards_and_nominations_received_by_James_Cameron \"List of awards and nominations received by James Cameron\")\n\n[![Image 81: Cameron receiving a star on the Hollywood Walk of Fame in December 2009](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a5/JamesCameronStarDec09.jpg/250px-JamesCameronStarDec09.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:JamesCameronStarDec09.jpg)\n\nCameron receiving a star on the [Hollywood Walk of Fame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame \"Hollywood Walk of Fame\"), December 2009\n\nCameron received the inaugural [Ray Bradbury Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ray_Bradbury_Award \"Ray Bradbury Award\") from the [Science Fiction and Fantasy Writers of America](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Science_Fiction_and_Fantasy_Writers_of_America \"Science Fiction and Fantasy Writers of America\") in 1992 for _Terminator 2: Judgment Day_.[[208]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-SFAwards-210) In recognition of \"a distinguished career as a Canadian filmmaker\", [Carleton University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carleton_University \"Carleton University\") awarded Cameron the honorary degree of Doctor of Fine Arts on June 13, 1998.[[209]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Barkman-2019-211) Cameron received the Golden Plate Award of the [American Academy of Achievement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_of_Achievement \"Academy of Achievement\") in 1998, presented by Awards Council member [George Lucas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Lucas \"George Lucas\").[[210]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-212) He also received an honorary doctorate in 1998 from [Brock University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brock_University \"Brock University\") in St. Catharines, Ontario, for his accomplishments in the international film industry.[[211]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-213) In 1998, Cameron attended a convocation to receive an honorary degree from [Ryerson University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toronto_Metropolitan_University \"Toronto Metropolitan University\"), Toronto.[[209]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-Barkman-2019-211) The university awards its highest honor to those who have made extraordinary contributions in Canada or internationally. A year later, Cameron received the honorary Doctor of Fine Arts degree from [California State University, Fullerton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/California_State_University,_Fullerton \"California State University, Fullerton\").[[212]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-214) He accepted the degree at the university's summer annual commencement exercise.[[213]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-215)\n\nCameron's work has been recognized by the [Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_of_Motion_Picture_Arts_and_Sciences \"Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences\"); as one of the few directors to have won three Academy Awards in a single year. For _Titanic_, he won Best Director, Best Picture (shared with Jon Landau) and Best Film Editing (shared with [Conrad Buff](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conrad_Buff_IV \"Conrad Buff IV\") and [Richard A. Harris](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richard_A._Harris \"Richard A. Harris\")). In 2009, he was nominated for awards in Best Film Editing (shared with [John Refoua](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Refoua \"John Refoua\") and [Stephen E. Rivkin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stephen_E._Rivkin \"Stephen E. Rivkin\"),[[214]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-216) Best Director and Best Picture for _Avatar._ Cameron has won two Golden Globes: [Best Director](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golden_Globe_Award_for_Best_Director \"Golden Globe Award for Best Director\") for _Titanic_ and _Avatar_.[[215]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-217)\n\nIn recognition of his contributions to underwater filming and remote vehicle technology, [University of Southampton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/University_of_Southampton \"University of Southampton\") awarded Cameron the honorary degree of doctor of the university in July 2004. Cameron accepted the award at the [National Oceanography Centre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Oceanography_Centre \"National Oceanography Centre\").[[216]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-218) In 2008, Cameron received a star on [Canada's Walk of Fame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canada%27s_Walk_of_Fame \"Canada's Walk of Fame\")[[217]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-219) and a year later, received the 2,396th star on the [Hollywood Walk of Fame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame \"Hollywood Walk of Fame\").[[218]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-220) On February 28, 2010, Cameron was honored with a [Visual Effects Society](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Visual_Effects_Society \"Visual Effects Society\") (VES) Lifetime Achievement Award.[[219]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-221) In June 2012, Cameron was inducted to The Science Fiction Hall of Fame at the [Museum of Pop Culture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museum_of_Pop_Culture \"Museum of Pop Culture\") for his contribution to the science fiction and fantasy field.[[220]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-sfhof2012-222) Cameron collaborated with [Walt Disney Imagineering](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walt_Disney_Imagineering \"Walt Disney Imagineering\") and served as a creative consultant on [Pandora – The World of _Avatar_](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pandora_%E2%80%93_The_World_of_Avatar \"Pandora – The World of Avatar\"), an _Avatar_-themed land at [Disney's Animal Kingdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disney%27s_Animal_Kingdom \"Disney's Animal Kingdom\") in [Florida](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lake_Buena_Vista,_Florida \"Lake Buena Vista, Florida\") which opened to the public on May 27, 2017.[[221]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-223)[[222]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-224) A species of frog, _[Pristimantis jamescameroni](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pristimantis\\_jamescameroni \"Pristimantis jamescameroni\"),_ was named after Cameron for his work in promoting environmental awareness and advocacy of [veganism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Veganism \"Veganism\").[[223]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-225)[[224]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-226)[[225]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-227)\n\nIn 2010, _Time_ magazine named Cameron one of the 100 most influential people in the world.[[226]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-228) That same year, he was ranked at the top of the list in _[The Guardian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\\_Guardian \"The Guardian\")_ Film Power 100[[227]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-229) and in 30th place in _[New Statesman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New\\_Statesman \"New Statesman\")_'s list of \"The World's 50 Most Influential Figures 2010\".[[228]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-230) In 2013, Cameron received the [Nierenberg Prize for Science in the Public](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nierenberg_Prize \"Nierenberg Prize\"), which is annually awarded by the [Scripps Institution of Oceanography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scripps_Institution_of_Oceanography \"Scripps Institution of Oceanography\").[[229]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-231) In 2019, Cameron was appointed as a Companion of the [Order of Canada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Canada \"Order of Canada\") by [Governor General](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Governor_General_of_Canada \"Governor General of Canada\")[Julie Payette](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Julie_Payette \"Julie Payette\"), giving him the [Post Nominal Letters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_post-nominal_letters_in_Canada \"List of post-nominal letters in Canada\") \"CC\" for life.[[230]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-232)\n\nIn 2020, Cameron was the subject of the second season of the Epicleff Media dramatic [podcast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Podcast \"Podcast\")_[Blockbuster](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blockbuster\\_(podcast) \"Blockbuster (podcast)\")_. The audio drama, created and narrated by [Emmy Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emmy_Awards \"Emmy Awards\")-winning journalist and filmmaker [Matt Schrader](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matt_Schrader \"Matt Schrader\"), chronicles Cameron's life and career (leading up to the creation and release of _Titanic_), and stars actor [Ross Marquand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ross_Marquand \"Ross Marquand\") in the lead voice role as Cameron.[[231]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-233)\n\nCameron was appointed as an Officer of the [Legion of Honour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Legion_of_Honour \"Legion of Honour\") in February 2025, the highest and most prestigious [order of merit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_merit \"Order of merit\") in France. He will officially be presented with the award at the [2025 United Nations Ocean Conference](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_United_Nations_Ocean_Conference \"2025 United Nations Ocean Conference\") in Nice, where he will be one of the guests of honour.[[232]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-234)\n\nIn 2026, he was named a recipient of the [Governor General's Performing Arts Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Governor_General%27s_Performing_Arts_Award \"Governor General's Performing Arts Award\").[[233]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_note-235)\n\nAwards and nominations received by Cameron's films | Year | Title | Academy Awards | BAFTA Awards | Golden Globe Awards December 2009)_. January 2010. After the success of The Terminator, Cameron was announced as writer and director of a $60 m big-screen adaptation of the comic book classic. But thanks to a tangled web of litigation, studio bankruptcies, and wrangling over screenplay credits, that movie never saw light ... What remains, however, is Cameron's tantalising 47-page Spidey 'scriptment', a compacted screenplay-cum-narrative that mapped out his entire film in brief ... Koepp's first draft is taken often word-for-word from Cameron's story, though later versions were heavily rewritten by numerous screenwriters. Despite this—and much to Cameron's chagrin— Koepp's name is the only one on the screenplay.\n67.   **[^](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Cameron#cite_ref-67)**[\"Who Is Spider-Man?\"](https://web.archive.org/web/20180303120956/http://www.hollywood.com/general/who-is-spider-man-57159110/). _Hollywood.com_. 输出 final answer。

**《泰坦尼克号》（1997）的导演是詹姆斯·卡梅隆（James Cameron），他执导的其他主要电影作品如下：**

### 剧情长片

| 年份 | 电影名称 | 中文译名 |
|:---:|:---|:---|
| 1982 | *Piranha II: The Spawning* | 《食人鱼2：繁殖》 |
| 1984 | *The Terminator* | 《终结者》 |
| 1986 | *Aliens* | 《异形2》 |
| 1989 | *The Abyss* | 《深渊》 |
| 1991 | *Terminator 2: Judgment Day* | 《终结者2：审判日》 |
| 1994 | *True Lies* | 《真实的谎言》 |
| 1997 | *Titanic* | 《泰坦尼克号》 |
| 2009 | *Avatar* | 《阿凡达》 |
| 2022 | *Avatar: The Way of Water* | 《阿凡达：水之道》 |
| 2025 | *Avatar: Fire and Ash* | 《阿凡达：火与烬》 |

### 纪录片

| 年份 | 电影名称 |
|:---:|:---|
| 2002 | *Expedition: Bismarck* |
| 2003 | *Ghosts of the Abyss* |
| 2005 | *Aliens of the Deep* |
| 2026 | *Billie Eilish – Hit Me Hard and Soft: The Tour (Live in 3D)*（音乐会3D电影） |

### 短片

| 年份 | 电影名称 |
|:---:|:---|

| 1996 | *T2-3D: Battle Across Time* |

此外，卡梅隆还编剧/制作了多部知名影片，如《异形：战斗天使》（2019）和《终结者：黑暗命运》（2019）等。
